### 160 subject events

- **1915.07** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Klara一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Josef Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Klara抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Klara哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Alois Weiss朝Klara招手。Klara挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Klara把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1918.08** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环在石板缝上颠了一下，Klara用钩子一拨，抢先绕过煤车。Maria Weiss 2跟在后面，铁环倒在地上转了两圈。三颗玻璃弹珠从Maria Weiss 2的口袋里摸出来，有一颗还带着体温。Maria Weiss 2把弹珠按进Klara手心，转身踢了一脚墙根。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood 0.05; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Helene Winter在绳子那头喊。Klara踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Helene Winter数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Klara把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1927.08** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social

  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  战时的野餐篮里只有黑面包、萝卜和一小块代用奶酪。Berta Weiss 3铺开毯子，说这地方以前是阅兵场。Gustav Lechner 2说现在也是，只是阅兵的人少了。远处有军乐队在排练，鼓点隔着雨传过来，像另一场野餐的餐具响。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +picnic_on_parade_ground; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; knowledge “the parade ground is now mostly empty”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Klara看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。

  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

- **1946.05** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Berta Weiss 3没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Klara听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1946.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Friedrich Hofer说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Friedrich Hofer陪着Klara去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-friedrich-hofer; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Anton Huber 3说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Klara站在原地看着Anton Huber 3拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; flag +uncertain_courtship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1947.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/split` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部在一次表决中分裂。Marie Huber带走了将近一半的人，留下的那间办公室突然显得很大。Klara把两把没人坐的椅子搬到墙边，没有扔掉。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.04999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009249_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010731_

- **1947.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/clean-audit` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Emil Lechner带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Klara把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Emil Lechner抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”_

- **1947.01** `r13-work.collect-debt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/debt-paid` tags: commerce, debt, collection, neighbor, conflict, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara第三次敲门时，Ferdinand Bauer才把门开了一条缝。屋里桌上摆着半块面包和一张当票。Klara没有进门，只把欠条摊在门框上。Ferdinand Bauer回屋翻了一阵，数出十克朗，硬币还带着体温；Klara在欠条背面写了“清”，把纸撕成两半。
  _effects: cash 10 (门口的三次敲门); flag +work13_collected_a_debt; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000288:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

- **1947.04** `r2-law.guardianship-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/denied-for-housing` tags: law, guardianship, family, court, housing, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Winter 2说住房太挤，监护权暂不批准。Klara问那孩子去哪里，Helene Winter 2说先由Alois Moser代管，等条件改善再申请。Maria Bauer 2后来送来孩子的成绩单，说换人签字以后，作业交得少了。
  _effects: condition; flag +guardianship_denied_for_housing; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; knowledge “the child stays with the alternate guardian until the housin”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1947.05** `r2-law.inheritance-tax-audit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/appealed-assessment` tags: law, inheritance, tax, audit, farm, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara对估价不服，找Katharina Huber 2写申诉，说母牛去年就死了。Helene Winter 2要求死牛证明；兽医的证明在村里，村长说档案柜的钥匙在Helene Winter 2手里。Klara跑了两趟，最后把申诉和一张村长签字的纸条一起递上去；Helene Winter 2收下，说等三个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (遗产清单上的犁（其中4克朗记入债务）); flag +appealed_inheritance_tax_assessment; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; knowledge “the village records are locked in the auditor’s own cabinet”_

- **1948.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/signed-for-a-favor` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Anna Winter 2在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Klara把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: organization_funds; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

- **1948.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/soup-and-grave` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  忌日那天，Klara在教堂旁的饭桌上先给Elisabeth Bauer盛了一碗汤，把分家时那只柜子的事提了出来，说完就不再辩解。Elisabeth Bauer用勺子搅了两圈，讲了自己当时欠的账。饭吃到一半，两人一起去墓地，路上买了半克朗的蜡烛。
  _effects: relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; cash -0.5 (墓前的蜡烛); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1948.05** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Klara把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Maria 2替Klara扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001805:child_

- **1948.08** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1948.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1948.11** `r2-faith.cooperative-dividend::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/dividend-paid` tags: cooperative, dividend, shop, board, suspicion, vienna, urban, postwar
  分红按购货额算，Klara一年买了四十七克朗的杂货，分到三克朗二十赫勒。Alois Weiss把硬币数在柜台上，说今年比去年少，因为对面新开了平价铺。Klara把钱又放回柜台，换了两袋面粉和一罐糖。
  _effects: cash 3 (合作社的分红日); flag +received_cooperative_dividend; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; knowledge “the dividend is calculated from annual purchases”_

- **1948.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1949.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/waiting-list` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  Klara去住房局问结果，办事员翻过卡片说分数不够：家里人口少，现住房虽挤但还没到拆除标准。回程的电车上，Katharina Weiss 2劝他明年再递一次申请。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (application and tram fare（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/refuse-push-bill` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara把药费单从糖罐底下抽出来，对折，推回桌子中央，说上个月的工钱已经贴过煤钱，这回一个赫勒也拿不出。Josef的脸沉下来，练习本翻得哗哗响，最后把单子重新压回糖罐底下，起身去够门后的外套。三只咖啡杯里的渣子凉在桌上，兄妹这一回把话都说薄了。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/violets-on-the-step` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰（其中0.24克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1949.02** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1949.05** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.ask-straight` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara在楼梯间拦住Mitzi Weiss，问那句话是从哪儿听来的，又趁送活的人上门时当面看清是谁。原来是对面作坊的裁缝来取改好的裤子。Mitzi Weiss听完把话咽回去，第二天洗衣房里没人再提。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000620:child_

- **1949.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Klara胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

  Klara从内袋里数出两克朗压在柜台上，Josef Kral翻开借书卡，用铅笔把上次迟还的四天圈出来，还是把小说递了过来。Klara把书塞进外套里侧，出门就翻到第一页，边走边读，差点撞上推手推车的菜贩；两克朗换来的不只是一本小说，还有一整个下午的安静。
  _effects: cash -2 (租书店借小说的押金); skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中1.6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08000000000000002 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1914.06** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/wet-floor` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Alois Lechner端盆出去倒水，灶边淌了一地。Mitzi在摇床里翻身，摇床被湿地板滑得挪了位，撞在桌脚上。Alois Lechner回来时孩子哭得满脸通红，摇床腿松了一条。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (摇床撞上桌脚，受了惊); cash -0.3 (请楼下木匠紧摇床腿); mood -0.030000000000000002_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Mitzi前面的大婶一直往后靠，Mitzi就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Mitzi的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1919.01** `kernel-social.first-meeting::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/first-meeting.school-bench` tags: social, friendship, first_meeting, childhood, neighbor, everyday, rural, agriculture
  学校的长凳不够，Mitzi和Rosa挤在一张桌上，用粉笔在桌角画了一道线。Rosa算术好，Mitzi会写花体字，两人交换着抄；放学后一起把老师的水罐提回厨房。那道线后来被袖子擦掉，谁也没有再画。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; skill; flag +school_friend_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Ferdinand Kral把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Mitzi伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Ferdinand Kral从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Mitzi手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Mitzi一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  _effects: skill; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +learned_table_order_

  Mitzi路过糖果铺，用买盐的钱买了两颗糖。盐只买了半包，Alois Lechner一尝就尝出来。Alois Lechner让Mitzi把糖纸交出来，摊在桌上，说这下全家都要少放盐。Mitzi把没吃的那颗推回去，糖纸粘在掌心。

- **1927.01** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-friend-moves-away` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa的父亲在车站找到活，全家要搬去另一区。临走前，Rosa把一本旧书塞给Mitzi，扉页上写着地址。Mitzi把书读完，按地址写了信，信被退回来，上面盖着“查无此人”。那本旧书Mitzi一直留在书架上。
  _effects: relation_end friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “朋友搬走前留下的地址已经找不到人了。”; skill; flag +friend_moved_away; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  Mitzi问了三家印刷厂，得到的回答都是“今年不收学徒”。最后一家让他把名字写在纸角上，说有空缺再通知，但谁都知道那张纸会被压在账本下面。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; knowledge “今年印刷厂大多不收新学徒。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  Mitzi还是把这件事告诉了母亲。母亲没多问，在厨房案板旁多摆了一副碗筷，晚上让Elisabeth Bauer睡在炉边的旧垫子上，说先住下，别的事明早再想。
  _effects: relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; food; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

  Josef Bauer把Mitzi的名字写进一本硬皮簿子，问一句写一句，最后让他按手印。Alois Lechner赶来时，Josef Bauer说带回去好好管教，这次不立案。走出门口，Alois Lechner的手一直攥着Mitzi的袖子。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”_

- **1927.07** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Mitzi的手，又让Mitzi推两下刨子试试。Mitzi用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Alois Lechner，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  Ferdinand Lechner把一条绷带绕在Mitzi的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Mitzi在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Ferdinand Lechner发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

  Mitzi把一半的钱推过去，说剩下的下周补。Josefine Hofer没有接，先问是不是厂里又短了工钱。Mitzi点头。Josefine Hofer把收据写成两张，一张给现在，一张压在茶杯下面。
  _effects: cash -3 (第一张房租收据（其中1.6克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +short_first_rent; relation rival with person-josefine-hofer_

  Mitzi没有寄信，把信塞进Anna Bauer常去的书店，夹在一本旧书里。三个月后，书被卖掉，信也不见了；Katharina Huber说那家书店上个月清过仓，旧书论斤卖。Mitzi去废纸站找了一下午，只找到半张写着我不敢寄的纸。
  _effects: cash -1 (寄给作者的信); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +hid_letter_in_bookshop; knowledge “the bookshop sold its old stock by weight”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  夜校的名额满了，登记的人让下个季度再来。Alois Lechner把报名表折好，说本来也不是非学不可；Mitzi看见Alois Lechner把那张纸夹进了祈祷书。回家的电车上，Alois Lechner一直看着窗外的招牌，忽然问Mitzi那个字念什么。
  _effects: flag +night_school_waitlist; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the parent keeps the enrolment form in the prayer book”; condition_

- **1930.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/wage-question-silence` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  Alois Lechner问起工钱和房租，Mitzi报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Elisabeth Bauer 2低着头把面包掰成小块，Alois Lechner往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1930.07** `kernel-social.tenement-rumor::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tenement-rumor.station-story` tags: social, rumor, reputation, neighbor, housing, everyday, reform, medical
  门房说Mitzi家的女儿在车站跟一个穿军装的人上了车。话传到三楼时，女儿还没回家；Mitzi去问Grete Berg，Grete Berg说只是看见她替人提箱子。Maria Kral 3在厨房门口听着，把面包掰成两半，谁也没接。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; role; flag +rumor_met; knowledge “话是从洗衣房传出来的，门房只是把它说给了三楼。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1930.09** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction

  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +wish:knowledge:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003392_

- **1930.10** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Ferdinand Lechner却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Mitzi把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1930.11** `r1-hc.home-fever::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fever-breaks` tags: health, fever, home, care, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  夜里Mitzi烧到四十度，Grete Berg把醋倒在毛巾上，一遍遍擦胳膊窝。窗玻璃结着霜，炉上的水壶却叫个不停。第三天早晨Mitzi自己坐起来要水喝，嘴唇裂了口，第一句话是问今天谁去上工。
  _effects: cash -3 (水银停在四十度（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; knowledge “vinegar rubs and steam were the whole treatment at home”_

- **1931.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  药房在街口，柜台玻璃下面压着价目纸。Mitzi把两克朗推过去，伙计找回来十二赫勒，药片用报纸包成一个小包。工友接过去时手还在抖，报纸边角很快被汗洇软。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

  Ferdinand Berg把签名纸翻过来，说地址不全，有几页只写了楼号没有门牌。Mitzi说那些人怕报复，Ferdinand Berg说怕报复就别签名。纸被退回来，边角已经卷了。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +petition_rejected_for_missing_addresses; knowledge “the office rejects petitions without a full address for ever”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1931.08** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-borrow-from-neighbor` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  看门人正把垃圾桶往院角推，见你回来，眼神在你和门牌之间转了一圈。房东收下四克朗，在账本上划掉一行，没提换锁的事。你上楼时腿有点发软，楼梯扶手凉得扎手。
  _effects: cash 4 (向邻居借来补交欠租); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; mood 0.03999999999999998_

- **1931.09** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi和Ferdinand Kral谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Mitzi家，钥匙只在白天给。Ferdinand Kral说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Ferdinand Lechner听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Mitzi把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.10** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social
  欠账拖了半个月。玛丽亚在菜摊前看见Mitzi路过，直接喊住要钱。Mitzi拿不出，玛丽亚把已经称好的土豆倒回筐里。Katharina Moser 2提着篮子站在两步外，什么也没说。
  _effects: food; reputation -0.15; mood -0.10000000000000003; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/public-suspicion` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  楼里的邻居开始议论Mitzi和Katharina Stein。有人把门牌记下来，房东来敲门，说这栋楼不接受“这种住法”；Katharina Stein把箱子拖出来又推回床下，搬家花掉五克朗，两人那一夜都没睡稳。Mitzi在院子里被人盯着看，门上的名字也被人用手指划过。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; reputation -0.12000000000000002; mood -0.09999999999999998; cash -5 (moving and lost rent); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

  Hermann Weiss 2只肯借九克朗，还要Mitzi把工钱袋每月先送到柜台。Hermann Kral 2说这条件比利息还重，Hermann Weiss 2把借据折起来，说那就请便。Mitzi追到门口，还是把九克朗收下了，纸币上有一股烟草和樟脑混在一起的味道。
  _effects: cash 9 (借据上的第二行); condition; flag +took_small_loan; knowledge “the lender wants the wage packet delivered before rent”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1932.05** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Mitzi点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Hermann Kral 2量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  招工纸上的工钱写得明白，一天三克朗八十赫勒。Mitzi怕再等下去名额没了，从人缝里挤到前面，后面的Klara一把拽住袖子。门房抬头看了看乱糟糟的队伍，把玻璃窗拉上了。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Katharina Stein把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Mitzi说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Gustav Kral在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Mitzi的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; reputation -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  Maria Weiss 4把双方叫到楼下的门房，桌上摊着算到小数点后的账。Hermann Weiss 2同意再宽限半年，条件是Mitzi每周多还一克朗，并在借据背面写清违约的后果。Hermann Bauer端来两杯水，杯底沉着茶叶。Mitzi签字时，笔尖把“半年”两个字洇开了。
  _effects: cash -6 (到期日那天); condition; flag +loan_rescheduled; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; knowledge “the rescheduled loan costs one extra krone a week”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

  Ferdinand Berg把一张清单摊在钢琴上，说这间屋按“敌产”登记，家具要逐件编号。Mitzi说钢琴是母亲的，Ferdinand Berg说“那就写母亲的名字，东西还是不能动”。他在琴盖上贴了一张封条，封条压住了琴键。
  _effects: mood -0.16000000000000003; condition; flag +apartment_listed_as_enemy_property; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; knowledge “the piano is sealed under the enemy property inventory”_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wrong-grave` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, reform, medical
  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Maria Weiss忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Mitzi陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Maria Weiss站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

  Mitzi没入社，把蜜装进旧瓶子，挑到维也纳的市场上卖。价钱比合作社高，但来回走了一天，脚底磨出水泡。Otto Winter说这样卖不长久了。回到家时，鞋底已经磨穿。
  _effects: cash 4 (屋檐下的蜂群); flag +sold_honey_independently_

- **1933.09** `r2-rural.well-contamination::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-new-pump/refused-the-assessment` tags: rural, water, health, neighbor, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把分摊单推回去，说自家有井，凭什么替别人出钱。Ferdinand Berg在名单上划掉Mitzi的名字，泵还是装在了广场上。入冬后旧井又浑了，Mitzi提着桶去广场，排在Maria Weiss 2后面。
  _effects: reputation -0.04999999999999999; flag +refused_pump_assessment; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1933.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Friedrich Hofer在门口替Mitzi捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  把那张字据推回去，说房租和母亲的药费都在桌上摆着，这名字不能签。前两次的借条从证件夹里翻出来放在一起，摊平让对方看清。对方把纸卷起来收进外套，门带得比平常重，此后在街上碰见只当没认出来。
  _effects: reputation 0.02999999999999997_

- **1934.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Anna Huber点了牛奶咖啡，Mitzi要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

  Mitzi看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Grete Berg追出来问怎么不领了，Mitzi说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Mitzi把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: food_

  铅笔头短得捏不住，Mitzi把它夹在两指之间，在表格背面写下两个名字。被漏掉的人一个叫扬，一个叫弗兰茨，都是每天清早从火车站走来的临时工。工头看了一眼背面，没说话，把表贴回墙上。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Josef弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Josef把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Josef Bauer把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Josef每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.06_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Josef前面的大婶一直往后靠，Josef就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Josef的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1920.11** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Josef把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Emil挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Emil转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Josef站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.039999999999999994; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Josef站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Emil Weiss在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Josef掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Josef才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Josef的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Josef在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Luise Kral把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Josef伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Luise Kral从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Josef手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Josef一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1923.03** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Josef的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Josef追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Emil从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; condition_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +wish:craft:pursuing_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Josef在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Josef把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Josef下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance); role_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  床单叠成四方，压得筐绳勒进肩膀。Josef照着地址找到利奥波德城的两户人家，一户在院子深处，一户要上四层楼。按件算下来是一克朗六十赫勒，Josef把铜币一枚枚码在Klara枕边。Luise Kral在门口看见，只说“下回别敲错门”；家里省下的那份晚饭，让Klara多歇了一天。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (替妹妹送床单的计件工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (背着湿床单走了几个街区); relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; food_

- **1926.12** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/fined` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Friedrich Hofer说按条例罚三克朗，交不出就通知学校。Josef Bauer从内衣口袋里摸出叠好的钞票，一张张数，数到第三张时手停了。回家后，那三克朗被写在厨房的账单上，旁边画了一道红线。
  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.07999999999999999; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/joined-and-borrowed` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Josef跟着Maria Weiss 2走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.03999999999999998_

- **1927.07** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Anna Kral先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Josef每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:58; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.12** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.overtime-wage-scuffle/overtime-wage-scuffle.paid-short` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social, injury
  为赶制市议会选举的传单，Josef在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne连排三个晚上。结账时Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把加班算成“学徒练习”，只多给半克朗。Otto Weiss在楼梯间低声说这是常事，让Josef周末别来，去集市八角亭替人抄货单。
  _effects: cash 0.5 (三晚加班只补半克朗); health -0.050000000000000044 (连续三晚排字熬到十点); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1927.12** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “太小，手也薄。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，把Josef推过的木板翻过来，指着上面一道啃痕。Josef Bauer把布包往肩上提了提，说打扰了。Josef在门口多站了一会儿，直到里面的刨子声把话头盖过去，才跟着Josef Bauer下楼。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/hand-caught` tags: adolescence, labour, union, injury
  Josef在压纸时右手被滚轴卷进去，工头拉下电闸，血滴在铅字盘上。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让人把Josef送到医院，医药费先由厂里垫付。
  _effects: health -0.17999999999999994 (hand caught in press); condition; cash -4 (hospital transport)_

- **1928.01** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002884_

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1928.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/homesick-return` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef在寄宿学校住了不到一个月，夜里总听见火车声，想起家里的炉子和妹妹。Josef给Josef Bauer写信，写了撕、撕了写，最后只寄出一张明信片。Josef Bauer来接Josef那天，Josef的箱子还没完全打开。回到家，Josef把铁皮箱放在床底，再没打开，腌菜罐却一直留在桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1928.09** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.lead-type-drop/lead-type-drop.scolded` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social
  Josef在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne碰翻一整盘8点铅字，字块滚了一地。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne敲着桌沿骂人，扣下两个克朗。Anna Kral蹲在过道里帮忙捡字，只说了一句“下次用两只手托底”。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔偿散落铅字的工钱扣款); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1929.01** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  旧货贩子把外套翻过来看了看里布，伸出两个手指。Josef接过两枚克朗，塞进鞋垫底下，走回家时脚心一直硌着。晚上Josef Bauer站在门后，手搭在空衣钩上，问外套呢。Josef说不知道，声音比平时高了一点。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Josef废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.learn-the-task/demoted-to-clean` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  第一行排完，Josef自己看着都别扭：字距一宽一窄，像缺了牙。锁版时空铅掉出来三块，Alois Lechner 3把字盘摇了两下，什么都没说，只朝纸堆抬了抬下巴。Josef搬完纸又去洗墨辊，清洗剂熏得眼睛流泪，指甲缝黑了一个星期。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (清洗剂气味熏得他头晕); mood -0.050000000000000044; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1930.01** `p9-work-transport.strike-rumor/strike-rumor-keep-quiet` tags: work, factory, railway, transport, shift, labour, strike, rumor
  Josef什么也没说，照常上工。罢工那天厂门口只来了一半人，汽笛拉过两遍，车间里的皮带挂着没动。Josef Bauer在门房里点名，把到的人记下，把没到的人也记下。Josef站在车床前，听见外面远处的喊声，手里的活没停。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

  _effects: state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> failed-apprentice; flag +failed_apprenticeship; mood -0.07999999999999996; role_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/semmel-no-words` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  在柜台前买了两只 Semmel，递过一只，自己咬另一只，旧账一个字没提。对方接过面包，两人并肩站在店门口，把那点热气吃完。钱的事还横在中间，但对方说星期六照旧来喝咖啡，别又躲着。
  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel); food; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.08** `vienna-printshop-daily.overtime-order/overtime-accept` tags: work, factory, labour, social, injury
  油灯下Ferdinand Kral递过来一块干面包，Josef一边啃一边把纸喂进压印机。天蒙蒙亮时Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne数出几枚硬币拍在桌上，没多一句话。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (通宵加班费); health -0.050000000000000044 (通宵站立和油墨气味); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1930.10** `major-education-night-technical-school/night-school-certificate` tags: education, night_school, work, exhaustion, major
  夜校在第七区的旧校舍里，十二个工人挤在一张长桌前。Josef Bauer用粉笔画出齿轮的剖面，又让大家传看一只烧坏的保险盒。Josef把图纸带回家，在厨房桌上描到半夜。结业考试通过后，Josef拿到一张机械制图的证书，学费一共九克朗。
  _effects: education secondary; skill; cash -9 (evening-school fees); role; flag +night_school_certificate; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1931.01** `vienna-printshop-daily.wage-shortfall/wage-argue-and-win` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Ferdinand Kral和另外两个人跟着Josef去了办公室门口。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne最后从抽屉里摸出几枚硬币，边给边在本子上写了一行字。
  _effects: cash 1 (退还的部分扣款); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003469_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.repay-family-debt::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/debt-cleared` tags: task, commerce, debt, family, repayment, honour, reform, medical
  Josef把攒了半年的票子一张张抹平，当着Alois Lechner的面数清。Alois Lechner数到一半，把最后几张推回来，说利息就算了。Josef没有收，把钱重新推过去，说当初借的时候说好多少就是多少；Alois Lechner这才收下，把借条撕成两半。
  _effects: cash -12 (一克朗一克朗地数); flag +repaid_family_debt; role; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the old debt is finally cleared”_

- **1931.08** `kernel-social.shared-stove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/shared-stove.scald` tags: social, neighbor, everyday, laundry, cooperation, conflict, reform, medical
  洗衣日，Emil Winter把铜锅坐在Josef的火眼上，水开时溢出来，浇灭了另一边的火。Josef伸手去端锅，手背烫红了一片；Emil Winter把锅端开，去药房买回一小盒烫伤膏。回来时，铜锅被刷干净放在门口。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (scalded hand at the shared stove); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; cash -3 (medicine and clean bandages); flag +stove_scald; knowledge “铜锅不能同时占两个火眼，开水溢出来会浇灭火。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1931.08** `kernel-social.neighbor-conflict::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/neighbor-conflict.coal-heap` tags: social, neighbor, conflict, everyday, housing, scarcity, reform, medical
  邻居把煤堆在走廊拐角，Josef的童车推不过去，只能把煤往墙边扫。邻居听见声响，出来说煤是自己的，谁动谁赔；Josef把扫帚靠在墙上，两人站在煤灰里，谁也不肯先回屋。Ferdinand Lechner在楼下听见，拿粉笔在地上画了一道线。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; role; cash -1 (a new broom for the coal dust); flag +corridor_feud; knowledge “门房在走廊地上画了线，煤堆不得越过。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1931.11** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Josef走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Josef不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Josef拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Josef的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Josef和Luise Kral在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Luise Kral把煤钱推过来，Josef接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.new-district-lodging::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/key-money-demanded` tags: social, housing, lodging, landlady, district, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Kral收了房租，又说得再付四克朗的“钥匙钱”，否则前一个房客的家具不搬走。Josef在门口站了一会儿，还是数了钱。进屋后才发现，床底下塞着前房客的一只破靴子。

- **1932.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/borrows_coal_risk` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Josef把自家煤箱里仅剩的四块煤分了两块给Maria Weiss 2，自己家那一夜没生火，裹着大衣睡的。孩子烧退了，Maria Weiss 2第二天还煤时多塞了一把粗盐。Josef当天下午就开始流清鼻涕，第三天咳得直不起腰。

  Hermann Bauer把Josef寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Josef追到码头时，Hermann Bauer已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Josef在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。

  _effects: employment Tagelöhner @ org-winter-print; role; cash 2 (first daily pay advance); relation colleague with person-otto-winter; flag +day_labour_only_

  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Josef把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Gustav Hofer站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Josef手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。

  Ferdinand Kral把Josef叫到停着的机器旁，让Josef把手放在进料口，感受木纹的走向。Ferdinand Kral说听声音比看尺子准，又让Josef闭眼听了一遍。下班时，Josef的袖口沾满机油，Ferdinand Kral把一块废砂布丢过来，说先擦手，再擦机器。
  _effects: flag +foreman_mentorship; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; knowledge “the grain tells the feed rate before the caliper does”_

  Luise Kral咳了半年，最后一个月连楼梯都上不去。Josef把床让给对方，自己睡在厨房的长凳上。Luise Kral死在清晨，Josef去市政办事处登记，又去Luise Kral上工的地方结清工钱；葬礼从简，神父念完经，Josef把Luise Kral的工票和剃刀收进一只铁盒。
  _effects: death (pulmonary tuberculosis); health -0.06000000000000005 (nursing and grief before the funeral); role; cash 8 (final wages collected from the partner's workplace); cash -12 (funeral, mass and black clothes)_

- **1935.01** `major-work-injury/injury-compensation-granted` tags: work, injury, disability, unemployment, major
  压板落下时，Josef的右手还在滚筒边。Helene Kraus拉下电闸，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头拿来急救箱，把两根手指包进纱布。医院截掉了指尖，事故报告写“操作不慎”；厂里先垫二十八克朗，六周后保险结算，厂里却通知Josef不用再来了，因为机器需要两只完整的手。
  _effects: health -0.21999999999999997 (crushed right hand); unemployment (no longer able to operate the press); cash 28 (accident insurance payment); role; flag +accident_compensation_

  半夜的空袭把屋顶掀开一角。Josef和邻居把家具拖到楼道，天亮后去市政办事处登记损失。窗户用木板钉上，一家人挤到地下室。

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/displaced` tags: war, liberation, 1945, migration
  炮火把Josef住的那条街烧掉一半。Josef跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `p10-migration-tasks.find-lodging/lodging-shared-bed` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, lodging, arrival
  他先租了合住的床位，一间屋三张床，和两个工人分摊炉煤。租金最便宜，押金只要两克朗，可夜里有人打呼、有人半夜回来踢到痰盂。他把工具袋压在枕头下，睡了第一夜。
  _effects: cash -3 (合住床位的押金与首周租金（其中3克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; knowledge “利奥波德城的合住床位一周约一克朗，押金两克朗”_

- **1945.02** `p10-family-fate.child-death/child-buried-hastily` tags: adulthood, family, marriage, birth, death, inheritance, household, housing
  办事处的人来核日期，指着册子说两处对不上，罚了钱才走。灶上药罐咕嘟作响，Josef用勺子压着药渣，听见里屋一阵猛咳。窗台上的蜡烛没吹灭，烧到只剩一截。
  _effects: death (measles); cash -6 (墓地和办事处的罚款（其中6克朗记入债务）); health -0.019999999999999997 (淋雨与悲伤之后高热十天)_

- **1945.04** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Josef去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1945.12** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-watched-tools` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  河面的雾散尽时，码头上的人走了一半。Josef蹲在石阶上替Gustav Lechner 2守着麻绳和铁钩，看装卸工在跳板上喊号子。汤送来时还烫嘴，豆子沉在碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009784_

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.join-choir::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/choir-language-split` tags: culture, choir, music, community, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  排练到一半，有人提出只唱德语，另一边的成员把歌本合上。Anna Bauer敲了敲谱架，说教堂不是议会。Elisabeth Moser站起来走了，Josef坐在原位，听见门在身后关上。
  _effects: flag +choir_split_over_language; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002853:child; knowledge “half the choir wants German only and the other half refuses”_

- **1946.04** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010355_

- **1946.05** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.delay` tags: home, market, food, social
  三个克朗拖了九天。玛丽亚收下时数都没数，只把Josef的名字从木牌上抹去，粉笔灰落在卷心菜上。Rosa Kral拎着篮子从旁边过去，没停脚。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002828:child_

- **1946.11** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.moving-job/moving-heavy-books-bonus` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, moving, neighbor
  书箱用麻绳捆了两道，抬起来绳子往肉里勒。Marie Huber在前头喊慢点，Josef闷着头下楼梯，数着台阶。Josef Moser 2在院子里看着，等箱子放稳，从马甲口袋里摸出几个赫勒。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (搬书箱额外给的辛苦钱)_

- **1946.11** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.repair` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  Helene Winter 2从柜子里拿出攒了三个月的零钱袋，倒在桌上，说先给Josef买双能进厂的结实靴子。Josef数出鞋匠要的数目，把剩下的又推回袋子中央。
  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴（其中2.4克朗记入债务）); health 0.019999999999999997 (脚上有了不漏水的靴子); relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1946.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  铅笔头短得捏不住，Josef把它夹在两指之间，在表格背面写下两个名字。被漏掉的人一个叫扬，一个叫弗兰茨，都是每天清早从火车站走来的临时工。工头看了一眼背面，没说话，把表贴回墙上。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; reputation 0.04_

- **1947.01** `r13-fam.share-budget::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/separate-purses` tags: task, family, budget, money, household, trust, vienna, urban
  Helene Winter 2不肯把钱并在一起，说各人的工钱各人管，房租对半分。Josef把已经分好的煤钱又推回去一半，铁盒留在桌上，谁也没有去拿。那天晚上，两人各数各的铜板，灯芯剪了两次。
  _effects: condition; flag +kept_separate_money; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1947.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Josef把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +union_ledger_public; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1947.12** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/queue-until-noon` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Josef在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Josef去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Josef数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”_

- **1948.01** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/sold-a-secret` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Weiss 3把Josef在饭桌上说过的名字和地址卖给了别人。几天后，Josef发现自己的话出现在一封不该出现的信里。Berta Weiss 3没有否认，只说“大家都要活”。
  _effects: flag +betrayed_after_trusting; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:299; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130_

- **1948.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Josef的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Josef被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”_

- **1948.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Josef听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Alois Weiss扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛)_

- **1948.11** `r3-mig.passport-queue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-passport-exchange/bought-a-place-in-line` tags: migration, papers, queue, bureaucracy, work, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Weiss在队里替人占位，一个位子六克朗。Josef把钱塞过去，下午就站到了窗口前。Grete Weiss翻到出生地一栏，说拼写和档案不一致，要回原籍开证明。Luise Weiss在门外等着分钱，听见这话，先把六克朗往口袋里按了按。
  _effects: cash -6 (雨里的护照队（其中4.5克朗记入债务）); flag +bought_queue_place; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000345:child; knowledge “a queue place costs six crowns”_

  调查员又来找Josef谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 15 -> 30_

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1949.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/neighbor-soup` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Marie Huber端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Josef家的炉子捅旺。Josef靠在床头，听Marie Huber讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.04 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-marie-huber_

- **1949.09** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; reputation -0.03_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Josef谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。

- **1950.01** `r13-civ.buy-medicine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/half-bottle` tags: health, medicine, pharmacy, cash, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef只买得起半瓶，Alois Hofer 2把大瓶倒进小瓶，塞紧软木塞，说省着喝，别兑水。回家路上他把瓶子贴在胸口，怕冻裂。第三天咳嗽轻了，药也见底了。
  _effects: cash -2 (药柜上的两瓶糖浆（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.020000000000000004 (药柜上的两瓶糖浆); flag +bought_half_medicine_course; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

- **1950.03** `p9-history-union-political.meeting/meeting-speak-up` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Josef站起来报出自己车间的计件工价：糊一百个纸盒二十八赫勒，比隔壁车间少六个。屋里一下子静了，Mitzi Weiss把草稿推过来，让书记员把这几个数字写在动议第一行。散会时有人拍了拍Josef的肩膀。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000620:child_

- **1950.09** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Emil Weiss把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Emil Weiss在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1950.09** `kernel-health.clinic-queue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital::clinic-queue-reform-clinic/clinic-reform-insured` tags: health, clinic, queue, medical, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  保险诊所的窗口先查缴费卡。Josef的卡片边缘磨白了，办事员仍盖了章：挂号一先令，药费另算。医生看得很快，但至少把听诊器捂热了才贴上来。药费单子要另排一次队，窗口下午三点就关。
  _effects: cash -1 (insurance clinic registration fee（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.019999999999999997 (examined and treated at the insurance clinic); role; flag +krankenkasse_card; knowledge “保险诊所挂号只要一先令，但药费另算，缴费记录断了就得先补缴。”_

- **1950.10** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/quiet-solidarity` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Josef没有发言，只把带来的面包分成几份。散会时，一个不认识的女人把她的地址塞给Josef，说那条街还需要有人继续收会费。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003067:child_

- **1951.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Josef一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Marie Huber送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

- **1951.01** `state_expire:under-investigation` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick, state:under-investigation
  Josef的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:299_

- **1951.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.give-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Josef只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Josef把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Josef站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.03; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.07** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef和Maria Kral谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef家，钥匙只在白天给。Maria Kral说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Otto Kral听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004520:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.12** `r2-leisure.mourning-dress::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::mourning-dress-reform/altered-ready-made-mourning-dress` tags: leisure, dressmaker, mourning, grief, craft, vienna, urban, postwar
  成衣店的黑裙第二天就送到，Marie Huber穿着来改腰。Josef拆开下摆，发现里面缝着一块旧衬，针脚是殡仪馆的手法。Marie Huber问要不要拆掉，Josef说拆了就不挺了，留着吧，反正没人看见。
  _effects: cash 3 (黑绸裙的最后一针); condition; flag +altered_ready_made_mourning_dress; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; knowledge “the ready-made mourning dress is lined with funeral-contract”_

- **1951.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Josef把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002226:child_

- **1952.01** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  汤锅冒着热气，Josef盛出两勺倒进Helene Winter 2带来的罐子里。Helene Winter 2说了声谢谢就匆匆上楼。隔天罐子还回来，锅底搁着一小块猪油。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1952.01** `r13-civ.dentist::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/tooth-pulled` tags: health, dentist, pain, cash, craft, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Hofer让Josef咬住一块木楔，把钳子伸进嘴里，脚蹬着椅背使劲。牙根断在牙龈里，Gustav Hofer又换了一把小凿子。出门时Josef的半个脸已经肿起来，雪落在脸上，反而觉得凉得舒服。
  _effects: cash -2 (拔牙钳与门外的雪（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +tooth_extracted; knowledge “the tooth root broke and had to be chiselled out”_

- **1952.02** `r3-war.field-dressing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital::dressing-station-recheck/learned-proper-bandaging` tags: war, army, medicine, first_aid, wound, vienna, urban, postwar
  到了救护所，Otto Weiss把Josef的绷带拆开重包，说结打得太紧，脚趾会坏死。Josef蹲在门口看，记住了他先垫棉花再缠的做法。Berta Weiss 3被推进手术室前，把沾血的皮带解下来塞给Josef，说“下次还你一条新的”。
  _effects: flag +learned_proper_bandaging; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

  Josef数出八赫勒交给Anton Weiss，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Anton Weiss说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Josef盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1916.06** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/wooden-horse` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Alois Lechner把报纸包推到Rosa面前，包得紧，Rosa拆到第三层才看见木头。木马只有巴掌大，四条腿磨得圆滑，鬃毛是一刀一刀刻出来的。Rosa把它揣进衣襟，上楼时用手护着，晚上放在枕头边，袖口蹭了一道黑印。
  _effects: mood 0.08; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Mitzi Berg在绳子那头喊。Rosa踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Mitzi Berg数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Rosa把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.03 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1919.01** `kernel-social.first-meeting::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/first-meeting.coal-basket` tags: social, friendship, first_meeting, childhood, neighbor, everyday, rural, agriculture

  Rosa蹲在面包房后门的台阶上，手指抠着筐沿。Emil Weiss提着篮子过来，脚步很轻，掰下半个黑面包塞进Rosa手里，说“别让里面的人看见”。Rosa咬了两口，把剩下的包进纸里，回家放在桌上。

- **1929.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick

  Rosa把借书证压在阅览室的台灯下，一页页抄书。Friedrich Hofer过来提醒闭馆，Rosa求了十分钟，把最后一章抄完。回家路上，Rosa把纸条贴在胸口，怕雪打湿，进门先烤干手才敢展开。第二天Rosa把抄本交给老师，老师翻了两页，说字比课文好看。
  _effects: flag +library_card; skill; knowledge “the library holds an 1890s survey of housing conditions”_

- **1929.05** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Rosa的手，又让Rosa推两下刨子试试。Rosa用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Alois Lechner，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  Rosa在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Rosa去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  Rosa的打字机老是卡键，修一次要两克朗。Rosa自己拆开，把纠缠的字锤一根根掰直，装回去时多出一颗螺丝。机器居然还能用，只是每次打到字母“e”都会顿一下，Rosa就用力按下去。Rosa把那颗多出的螺丝放进抽屉，没扔。
  _effects: cash -2 (打字机的字母盘（其中2克朗记入债务）); skill_

  _effects: cash -10 (工资扣款单（其中10克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +defaulted_on_credit_union_loan; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation rival with person-luise-kral; knowledge “a guarantor can be pursued when the borrower stops working”_

  _effects: food; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +ordinary_good_year_

- **1930.07** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; health -0.04000000000000001 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-luise-kral_

  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Emil踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Emil抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Emil举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Emil站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Hermann Kral在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Emil掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Emil才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Emil后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Emil没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Emil用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000006; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1920.08** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  煤车刚过去，路心还留着几块碎煤。Emil的铁环撞上去，弹到水沟边。Elisabeth Bauer先一步把铁环踩住，说要拿一颗石弹子来换。Emil蹲下去掏口袋，膝盖在石板棱上蹭掉一块皮，换回铁环时裤腿已经渗出血点；Emil没再争，只记住Elisabeth Bauer踩铁环的那只脚。
  _effects: mood -0.04; condition_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Maria Weiss 2家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Emil递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Maria Weiss 2端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Emil把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Emil后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Emil没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Emil用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Karl Kraus从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Emil。Emil把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; skill_

- **1923.11** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Emil把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Ferdinand Bauer挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Ferdinand Bauer转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Emil站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000288:child_

  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1924.07** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Emil把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Josef Bauer发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Emil都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.04_

  Emil把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Emil就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Emil还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Emil在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (考前最后一夜的煤油); mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Emil背着床单筐走过两条街，肩膀被带子磨出一道红印。第二户人家的女佣数了件数，把工钱用纸包好。Emil回家时Elisabeth Bauer还躺着，Emil把纸包塞到枕头底下，没叫醒人。晚饭时Elisabeth Bauer自己坐起来端碗，Emil才觉得肩膀没那么沉。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (替妹妹送床单的计件工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (背着湿床单走了几个街区); relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1926.03** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Anna Bauer说该自己走，Emil说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Emil摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.06999999999999999; health -0.030000000000000027 (在冰冷的楼梯上坐到半夜)_

  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Emil把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Emil那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (绷带练习); mood -0.06; flag +fainted_during_first_aid_demo; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  Emil去了Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne问工，得到的回答都是“下个月再来”。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Mitzi的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1930.01** `kernel-social.reconciliation::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reconciliation.shared-pot` tags: task, task:social, social, reconciliation, family, apology, conflict, reform
  Emil端着一锅炖肉去敲Anna Bauer的门，说肉炖多了，一个人吃不完。Anna Bauer让他进屋，把两只盘子摆在桌上；两人先谈煤价，再谈母亲的坟，最后才提那笔说不清的钱。Friedrich Hofer在楼下等消息，等到灯灭才走。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; cash -2 (meat and coal for the reconciliation supper); role; flag +family_reconciled; knowledge “两人把煤价、母亲的坟和那笔钱都谈到了。”_

- **1930.09** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Emil把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Emil读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-josef-bauer; flag +first_regular_job_

- **1930.10** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.listen-coins` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  琴箱的螺丝松了，每转一圈都发出吱呀声，可圆舞曲还是圆舞曲。她靠着墙听完，看见三楼窗台有人用绳子吊下一只小篮子收钱，绳子在风里晃。她摸出铜板时，猴子已经蹲在她脚边了。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Katharina Moser 2的母亲把Emil带来的点心原封退回，说家里不娶有肺病亲戚的人。点心是早上排队买的，油纸还热着，Emil把它抱在胸前。Katharina Moser 2站在门后没有出来，只让妹妹传话。Emil把戒指留在门房，走回Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时天还没黑，楼下的狗一直叫。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; role; flag +marriage_refused; cash -2 (pastries and the returned ring)_

- **1931.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing

  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖)_

  Emil在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Emil去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。

  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-berta-weiss_

  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_

  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Emil被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission（其中15克朗记入债务）); health 0.04999999999999999 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.02999999999999997 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -4 (药柜上的两瓶糖浆（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药柜上的两瓶糖浆); flag +bought_full_medicine_course; knowledge “the cough syrup must be taken three times a day”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

  _effects: condition; flag +sickened_by_polluted_ice; knowledge “the ice was cut from the canal near the slaughterhouse”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1935.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Lena咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Lena手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Maria Huber等在门口，问值不值，Lena只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价（其中0.1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.01999999999999999; skill_

  _effects: mood -0.16000000000000003; condition; flag +return_refused_party_card; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “an old party card blocks the return visa”_

  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Lena在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Lena去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1941.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hide-the-last-sack` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Lena把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Lena知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain_

- **1941.01** `r13-work.inherit-business::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/took-over-the-shop` tags: commerce, inheritance, family_firm, debt, succession, vienna, urban, crisis
  Friedrich Hofer把账本、印章和一把铜钥匙一起推到Lena面前，说铺子从今天起归Lena。账本最后一页记着欠供货商的钱，也记着三个老主顾的赊账。Rosa Stein站在柜台后面，等Lena开口；Lena先把印章收进内袋，再让Rosa Stein把今天卖出的货念一遍。
  _effects: cash 8 (父亲的账本和印章); flag +work13_inherited_family_business; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; employment Kaufmann @ org-wednesday-psychological-society; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1941.03** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria Kral这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 26; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007942_

- **1941.07** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Lena用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Berta Bauer 2翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Helene Bauer在走廊里问交了多少，Lena含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1941.07** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena被叫到学校，老师说Maria Kral这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Lena没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 26 -> 21; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 47 -> 52; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `kernel-work.task-creditor-composition::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/work-creditors-agreement` tags: task, work, business, bankruptcy, debt, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena把欠条、货单和钱箱里的零钱全摆在桌上，请Helene Winter 2做中间人。三家债主吵了一个下午，最后同意免掉利息，本金分六个月还。Josef Moser 2要Lena把进货单交出来查验，Alois Weiss则要一个签名。Lena签完字，钱箱里只剩十克朗。
  _effects: cash -10 (债务和解的首期付款（其中10克朗记入债务）); role; flag +creditors_agreement; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; knowledge “债主会议可以免息，但会要求交出全部进货单。”_

- **1942.06** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/the-witness-stays-quiet` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Berta Moser在办事处说了Lena一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Lena说一句话。几天后，Lena发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Berta Moser只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

- **1942.06** `p9-history-union-political.meeting/meeting-send-delegation` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Lena提议先派两个人去见厂主，谈不成再投票。Anna Bauer嫌太软，Alois Weiss却点头，说家里等着月底的工钱。最后表决，派代表的动议多出四票，Lena被记在代表名单的第二行。
  _effects: skill; flag +union_delegate_named_

- **1942.06** `r1-hc.crush-wall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory::crush-industrial-night-demolition/crush-night-beam` tags: work, accident, crush, demolition, injury, vienna, urban, crisis
  横梁落下来时，Lena只来得及把Maria 2推开。梁的一头压住她的小腿，另一头卡在砖堆上；十几个人用撬棍抬了三次，才把人拖出来。
  _effects: cash -12 (拆房队的墙先倒了（其中12克朗记入债务）); condition; role; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001805:child; move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1942.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena没有立刻答话，先去敲Emil Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Emil Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Lena把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1943.01** `kernel-work.task-household-debt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/work-debt-new-loan` tags: task, work, debt, household, credit, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena还不上旧账，只好向Helene Winter 2介绍的放贷人再借十克朗，月息两成。Alois Weiss收了旧账，又重新开了一页赊账；Lena在两本账上都按了手印。回家路上，Lena把两张借据分开藏，怕家里人看见。
  _effects: cash 10 (借新债还旧账); flag +new_debt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; knowledge “放贷人的月息是两成，借据要分开藏。”; condition_

- **1943.02** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/turn-away-with-fare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena直说家里腾不出地方，从抽屉里摸出半克朗车费和一块黑面包，塞给Emil Kral。Emil Kral的藤箱提手已经磨毛，在门槛上磕了一下。Lena关上门，听见脚步声沿楼梯往下，看门人在楼下问了一句，Emil Kral答得含含糊糊；厨房里少了一块面包，也少了一个本该住下的人。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (给表亲车费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1943.08** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.returned-sugar-box/sugar-box-go-in-person` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Lena亲自去Luise Weiss家，把话说清：糖盒既退回来，以后不再托人登门。Luise Weiss的母亲站在门里，说这都是为自家孩子打算，语气不重也不轻。Lena把帽子戴正，没有纠缠，下楼时一步一步数着台阶；门房和邻居都看在眼里，这一回至少把话说在了明处。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000345:child; reputation 0.03_

- **1943.09** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.listen-coins` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  琴箱的螺丝松了，每转一圈都发出吱呀声，可圆舞曲还是圆舞曲。她靠着墙听完，看见三楼窗台有人用绳子吊下一只小篮子收钱，绳子在风里晃。她摸出铜板时，猴子已经蹲在她脚边了。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1943.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Emil说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Emil陪着Lena去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1944.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/hide-the-last-sack` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Lena把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Lena知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Maria Kral的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Maria Kral今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 24 -> 32; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 49; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/mocked-by-younger-students` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena被年轻同学笑说口音重，念课文时全班哄笑。Lena把书合上，第二天没来。Maria Bauer 2托人带话，Lena只说家里忙，其实每天那个时辰都坐在河边，用树枝在泥地上划自己的名字。河水涨起来的那天，Lena在泥地上写的名字被冲掉了，Lena又写了一遍。
  _effects: mood -0.08999999999999997; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1944.02** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria Kral这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 21 -> 24; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009379_

- **1944.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.half-bag-with-note` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena只舀出半袋，说自家也不宽裕，要Maria Bauer 3把上次的煤钱一并记在纸上。Maria Bauer 3接过纸片，当着面把日期和数目重写一遍，两人各留一半。面粉少了，账却清楚了，Lena把纸片夹进账簿样的旧本子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1944.08** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Emil在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1944.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Lena在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中12克朗记入债务）); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  Lena在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: cash -4 (medicine（其中1.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1945.01** `r2-faith.orphanage-inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::r2-faith.orphanage-inspection-reform/hid-twelve-names` tags: charity, orphanage, inspection, children, coverup, vienna, urban, postwar
  新条例要求每张床之间留一臂宽，Rosa Stein连夜把十二个孩子送到乡下亲戚家。Lena在名册上看见十二个名字被蓝笔划掉，墨迹还没干。Luise Weiss量完最后一间房，说合格；Lena把名册合上，没有翻到那页。
  _effects: condition; flag +concealed_orphanage_names_from_inspection; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; knowledge “twelve children were moved out before the inspector arrived”_

- **1945.01** `r13-civ.ask-pharmacist::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/own-remedy` tags: health, pharmacy, advice, dosage, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Weiss 2推荐自家配的止咳膏，说比药房的糖浆管用，收了三克朗。膏体里有股焦糖味，Lena抹了两晚，胸口起了红疹。Alois Weiss 2说这是“发出来就好了”，又收了一克朗的洗剂钱。
  _effects: cash -4 (柜台后面的小秤（其中4克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +bought_pharmacist_own_remedy_

- **1945.04** `r3-ind.dock-gang::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/left-on-the-quay` tags: work, dock, casual_labour, hiring, union, vienna, urban, crisis
  号牌发到最后一块，Josef Moser 2把手里的空绳圈绕了两圈，说今天人够了。Lena在码头边站到中午，看着吊钩一趟趟从舱里提货。Berta Weiss 3收工时把自己的号牌翻过来给Lena看，背面刻着一道横线，说攒够十道就能排到前面。
  _effects: cash -2 (码头工班的号牌（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.01000000000000012; flag +left_on_the_quay_at_the_shape_up; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; knowledge “ten marks on a tally stick move a docker up the hiring line”; unemployment (no gang took the subject at the morning call)_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Maria Kral的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Maria Kral今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 35 -> 43; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 49 -> 46; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.ask-pharmacist::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/packets-sorted` tags: health, pharmacy, advice, dosage, trust, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena把三包药摊在柜台上，问哪一包是饭前吃。Grete Weiss没有抬头，先把小秤归零，再一包一包拆开闻，说其中一包是泻盐，不该和止咳药一起吞。Lena把药重新包好，手心全是纸屑。
  _effects: cash -2 (柜台后面的小秤（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (柜台后面的小秤); flag +pharmacist_checked_packets; knowledge “one packet was Epsom salt, not cough powder”_

- **1946.02** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria Kral这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 32 -> 35; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010465_

- **1946.07** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/not-king-material` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, vienna, urban
  Gustav Kral看了Lena的手，说这双手适合拿账本，不适合拿权杖。Lena回家路上买了一张最便宜的站票，在最后一排看完了整场戏；散场时，Lena记住的不是国王的台词，而是他坐下时椅子发出的声音。
  _effects: skill; cash -1 (买了一张站票); flag +cast_refused_

- **1946.07** `r2-uni.specimen-jar::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/kept-quiet` tags: science, medicine, specimen, error, teaching, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena把标签按回瓶身，糨糊还没有干。Marie Huber问有没有发现问题，Lena说没有。那天下午的示教课上，Anna Bauer指着瓶子说“这是肝”，Lena在笔记本上画了一个脾，又把它涂掉。
  _effects: flag +silent_specimen_error; knowledge “the professor demonstrated a spleen as a liver”_

- **1946.08** `recipe-health.pharmacy-mixup::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/reported-to-doctor` tags: health, medicine, error, institution, fear, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena没有拿药，直接去门诊找Maria Bauer 2。Maria Bauer 2听完，先问Lena记不记得瓶盖的颜色，又让Emil Kral把今天的处方簿送来。Emil Kral送来时，少了一页。
  _effects: flag +reported_pharmacy_error; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; knowledge “a prescription page is missing from the pharmacy book”_

- **1947.01** `r13-turn.recovery::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital::reform-public-clinic/fever-returned` tags: health, recovery, medicine, body, hope, vienna, urban, postwar
  绷带拆到第三层，伤口又肿了起来。Klara说是里面还有碎布，要再清一次。Lena问能不能不住院，Klara说不住院就得每天来换药。Lena算了算车钱和换药钱，最后选择隔天来，结果伤口拖了两个月才收口。
  _effects: cash -8 (拆掉绷带的那天（其中5克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +recovery_complicated; relation colleague with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1947.01** `r13-edu.nursing-course::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/finds-vein-first-try` tags: education, nursing, medicine, care, training, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena第一次给病人扎针，手抖得厉害，Maria Bauer 2让Lena先在自己胳膊上试。Lena扎了两次才回血，病人反倒安慰Lena。三个月后，Lena能闭着眼找到血管，白大褂口袋里总装着一小卷胶布和一块剪好的纱布。Maria Bauer 2在排班表上把Lena的名字往前挪了一格。
  _effects: cash 2 (病房里的第一针); flag +nursing_certificate; role; skill_
