- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Anton拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Gustav Kral弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Anton接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  Anton想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Alois Lechner听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Anton试着站起来，被Alois Lechner按回台阶上。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Luise Moser把Anton拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Anton把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Anton蹲下把两包并排，按Luise Moser说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; skill_

- **1923.05** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  “三张换一张。”Rosa把皇帝像在Anton眼前晃了晃。Anton数出三张攒了半年的烟画，一张张递过去。皇帝像到手还没捂热，Rosa已经推开杂货铺的门，用同一张卡换了糖。Anton把卡举到光下看，觉得皇帝的脸有点歪。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1923.10** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/strap-raised-then-dropped` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  皮带从门后抽出来，Josefine Hofer举到半空，停住了。Anton闭着眼等，只听见皮带扣碰在桌沿上。Josefine Hofer把它放下，说晚饭后不许出门，把碎碗片捡干净。Anton蹲在地上一片片捡，窗外传来同伴的笑声，指尖被瓷片划出一道白口。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; health -0.010000000000000009 (手指被瓷片划破); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1925.01** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Anton把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Friedrich Hofer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Anton那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户刚把床单接过去，Ferdinand Berg就从楼梯口上来，盯着Anton看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Ferdinand Berg当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Hedwig的雇主。Anton站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Hedwig问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1926.01** `agency:seek_work` tags: agency, agency:seek_work
  Anton去了Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne问工，得到的回答都是“下个月再来”。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/damp-linen-returned` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Anton解释说路上没下雨，是床单叠得太厚。客户不听，Emil Winter也没帮腔，只把湿床单抖开，让Anton自己晾。Anton把布角一寸寸翻过来，跟Emil Winter说以后会分开叠、不让潮气闷在里头。Emil Winter哼了一声，算是记下了。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.03999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1927.11** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1927.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/joined-and-borrowed` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Anton跟着Emil走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1928.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1928.05** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1929.01** `agency:seek_work` tags: agency, agency:seek_work
  Anton去了Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne问工，得到的回答都是“下个月再来”。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/violets-on-the-step` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1930.02** `r2-rural.flood-meadow::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/saved-the-hay` tags: rural, flood, neighbor, institution, land, reform, medical, schooling
  河水漫过草滩时，Anton和Ferdinand Lechner把干草一叉一叉往高处扔。水到膝盖，靴子里灌满泥，走一步响一声。到半夜，草垛保住了，下游的磨坊却传来木头断裂的闷响。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (河滩上的水印); flag +saved_hay_from_flood; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1930.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Gustav Lechner点了牛奶咖啡，Anton要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1930.03** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Helene Winter把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Helene Winter在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1930.05** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Anton把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Katharina Moser 2站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Anton手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Anton上一个东家是谁。Anton说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Anton被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Anton站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Berta Weiss 3的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1931.01** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Emil Weiss没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Anton听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.arrange-childcare::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/childcare-arranged` tags: task, care, childcare, work, neighbor, wages, reform, medical
  Anton天没亮就把Maria 2裹好，抱到楼下Gustav Kral家。Gustav Kral接过孩子，先摸了摸尿布，又指给Anton看灶上温着的米汤。Anton把一星期的工钱数出一份放在柜上，Gustav Kral数也没数就收进围裙；出门时，Anton听见孩子在屋里哭了一声，又停了。
  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +arranged_daily_childcare; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; knowledge “the minder keeps a pot of rice water on the stove”_

- **1931.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Friedrich Hofer放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Berta Weiss 3在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Anton，Friedrich Hofer把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; food_

- **1932.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Berta Weiss 3没有答应，只说现在不合适。Anton把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003804_

- **1932.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.wait-for-the-brother` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Berta Hofer 2把话说得很慢：母亲还在，弟弟的学徒期还有三年。Anton点头，把准备好的结婚证词折起来放回口袋，那是借教堂的旧纸写的。两人在电车站分开，约好明年复活节再谈。Berta Hofer 2上车前替母亲拢了拢披肩，没有回头看。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; flag +engagement_postponed; cash -1 (tram fare to the meeting); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  _effects: cash -1 (板车与押金收据); reputation 0.05; flag +moved_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Maria Hofer排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Anton，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。

  Anton缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (穿着漏水的鞋在冷地上站久了); mood -0.03999999999999998_

  隔壁Franz Lechner 2的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Franz Lechner 2在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Anton下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Franz Lechner 2端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  Anton把空煤筐拎到隔壁门口，说只借一筐，等手头松了就还。Otto Winter看了看筐底的锈，进屋铲了半筐，又从灶边拿了两块引火的柴。Anton道谢时，Otto Winter说这条走廊里借东西要记着，不是记在纸上。

  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:100; cash 2 (首演酬劳); role; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.039999999999999994_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unfinished_coat_

  Anton在人群外站到腿发酸，只隔着跳舞的人看了Alois Moser几眼。散场时Luise Weiss裹着围巾凑过来，Anton低声托Luise Weiss去打听Alois Moser的门第和姓氏，自己先出了酒馆，在冷风里等回话；一整晚没敢上前，鞋底粘着酒馆地板的糖渍。

  教父Helene Winter的房东把阁楼收回，Anton把自家床边的空地量了量，还是让教父把箱子搬进来。Anton在床和墙之间搭了一块木板，夜里翻身会碰到箱子；房东来收租时看见多了一个人，说下个月要加两克朗。

- **1934.06** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard, injury

  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (被楼下争吵搅得没睡好); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.10** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Anton回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.039999999999999925 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Hermann Kral把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Hedwig伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Hermann Kral从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Hedwig手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Hedwig一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work

  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_
